# Prebuild report V2: every indexable page (branch seo-prebuild)

Built HTML from the prebuild (served by next start) joined to the approved keyword map, using the NEW keyword URLs. 3007 indexable URLs. Full per-URL data with every count: docs/seo/PREBUILD-FULL-REPORT-V2.csv. Social profile links are the 8 profiles in the footer; the last column of each row shows how many times each profile link appears on that page. Nothing is committed.

## 2. How to read the per-page tables

Each row: slug (URL) | page | primary keyword [intent volume/KD] (name-based = no Semrush data) | SEO title | meta description | primary counts: title/meta/H1/H2-H3/first 10%/FAQ/alt/body and density | secondary keywords [intent vol/KD] with body count and where found (M=meta, H=H2/H3, F=FAQ, A=image alt).
